([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentdb-learning/skills/agentdb-feedback) · ★107)
- **agentdb-graph-partition**: Partition or prune an AgentDB graph in code — exact minimum cut with MincutService (backed by @ruvector/mincut-wasm) and Personalized PageRank sparsification with SparsificationService (backed by @ruvector/graph-transformer). Use when splitting a memory or causal graph at its weakest link, finding the bottleneck between two clusters, keeping only the nodes most relevant to a seed node before attention, or when the user asks which native graph packages AgentDB uses. ([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agentdb-graph-partition) · ★107 · 2 copies)
- **agentdb-hierarchical-store**: Store memories in tier-aware hierarchical memory — working / short-term / long-term — and recall with tier filters. Use for working-set context that should fade, vs facts that should persist, vs patterns that should be searchable forever. ([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentdb-causal/skills/agentdb-hierarchical-store) · ★107)
- **agentdb-hybrid-search**: Hybrid search — BM25 keyword + dense vector fused with Reciprocal Rank Fusion. Use when queries have specific identifiers, code symbols, or proper nouns that pure semantic search might miss. ([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentdb-search/skills/agentdb-hybrid-search) · ★107)
- **agentdb-hyperedge**: Search and manage hyperedges — n-ary relationships between memories. Use for swarm membership, multi-cause incidents, or any "this involves all of (A, B, C, D)" relationship that doesn't fit a binary edge. ([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentdb-graph/skills/agentdb-hyperedge) · ★107)
- **agentdb-init**: Initialize an AgentDB Cognitive Container (.rvf file) in the current project. Sets up storage, embedder config, and the agentdb MCP server. Use when the user is starting a new project that needs vector memory, or asks to "set up agentdb" / "init agentdb". ([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentdb-core/skills/agentdb-init) · ★107)
- **agentdb-learn**: Train one of AgentDB's 9 RL algorithms on a stream of episodes. Use when the user has accumulated successful/failed episodes and wants to derive a policy, or when a task type is repeated enough to benefit from RL routing. ([ruvnet/agentdb](https://github.com/ruvnet/agentdb/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentdb-learning/skills/agentdb-learn) · ★107)
- **agentdb-learning**: Create and train AI learning plugins with AgentDB's 9 reinforcement learning algorithms. Includes Decision Transformer, Q-Learning, SARSA, Actor-Critic, and more. Use when building self-learning agents, implementing RL, or optimizing agent behavior through experience. ([ruvnet/ruflo](https://github.com/ruvnet/ruflo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/agentdb-learning) · ★82k · 13 copies)

- **agentdescent**: Improve a SKILL.md, agent definition, system prompt, small codebase or host plugin by measuring it against examples and evolving it, rather than rewriting it by hand and hoping. Use whenever the user asks to improve, fix, tune, optimise, "train" or get better results out of one of those -- including when they have no test cases yet, because drafting cases for them to check is step one of the procedure, not a prerequisite for it. - **agentdev-workflow-case-ready**: case-ready workflow 本体。Definition PR 受入、canonical Definition 再取得、Decision 受理、execution contract と Standard/Epic 構造確定、traceability gate、ready 遷移、draft/RU 削除、冪等再実行を制御する。USE FOR: case-ready の工程制御。DO NOT USE FOR: 単独起動（case-auto 内部段階）、Root Case/Definition PR 作成（case-open）、実装（case-run）、PR 完了判定（case-close）。 ([yogata/agent-dev-flow](https://github.com/yogata/agent-dev-flow/tree/HEAD/src/common/skills/agentdev-workflow-case-ready))
- **agentdev-workflow-case-revise**: 内部 lifecycle 段階 case-revise の workflow 実装本体。再合意済み Definition 変更の受入確認（未合意変更の req-define 差し戻し）、canonical Definition との実変更判定、冪等キーによる既存 Definition Amendment PR の検出と再利用、設計修正PR作成、Epic 完了済み Issue の影響再評価（巻き戻し禁止）、合意変更の受領確認経路、Case 関連 Issue 本文更新、case-ready 引き継ぎ、中断済み成果物の再利用による収束を所有する。USE FOR: case-revise 実行時の workflow 制御。DO NOT USE FOR: 新しい要求・Decision・対象範囲の決定と Definition の意味判断（req-define 側の責務）、execution contract / execution structure の再確定（case-ready 側の責務）、実装実行（case-run 側の責務）、単独起動（case-auto の内部 lifecycle orchestration から起動される内部段階である）。 ([yogata/agent-dev-flow](https://github.com/yogata/agent-dev-flow/tree/HEAD/src/common/skills/agentdev-workflow-case-revise))
- **agentdev-workflow-case-run**: 内部 lifecycle 段階 case-run の workflow 実装本体。単一 Issue 実行への収斂（実行担当サブエージェント委譲1件、result 4状態処理、前置/最終 gate）を所有する。Epic・Wave を処理対象とする実行契約は廃止され、Wave 実行制御は case-auto の orchestration stage 3 が単一所有する。USE FOR: case-run 実行時の workflow 制御（単一 Issue 実行・再開フェーズ判定・委譲・前置/最終 gate）。DO NOT USE FOR: 実装実行そのもの（委譲内の実行担当サブエージェントが担う）、Epic や Wave の実行制御（case-auto orchestration stage 3 の責務）、単独起動（case-auto の内部 lifecycle orchestration から起動される内部段階である）。 ([yogata/agent-dev-flow](https://github.com/yogata/agent-dev-flow/tree/HEAD/src/common/skills/agentdev-workflow-case-run))

- **agentdev-workflow-phase-review**: phase-review command workflow。現在の正規成果物群・既定要求・検証証拠を照合する独立レビューと、工程全体像の再構成出力の実装本体。独立レビューは所見を確定違反・証拠不足・推測・未採用改善候補の4区分で提示し、工程全体像は採用済み事項・必要な上流前提・未決事項を区別した閲覧像として導出する。工程の完了を認定せず、閲覧像を第二の正本として保存しない。USE FOR: phase-review 実行時の照合対象解決、所見区分判定、工程全体像の再構成出力、提示形式の適用。DO NOT USE FOR: 正規成果物の更新、所見の intake/learning 自動投入、工程完了の認定、遷移操作（agentdev-workflow-phase-transition）、テーマ形成・更新・成果物反映（各 phase-tasks/phase-artifacts workflow）、単独起動（対応する /agentdev/phase-review コマンド経由で利用すること）。 ([yogata/agent-dev-flow](https://github.com/yogata/agent-dev-flow/tree/HEAD/src/common/skills/agentdev-workflow-phase-review))
